{baseline_end.date()}\")\nprint(f\"Treatment period: {treatment_start.date()} to {treatment_end.date()}\")\n\n# Add period labels\npurchases_clean['Period'] = purchases_clean['Order Date'].apply(\n    lambda x: 'baseline' if baseline_start <= x <= baseline_end\n    else 'treatment' if treatment_start <= x <= treatment_end\n    else 'other'\n)\n\n# Filter to baseline and treatment periods only\nanalysis_data = purchases_clean[purchases_clean['Period'].isin(['baseline', 'treatment'])].copy()\nprint(f\"Data for analysis: {analysis_data.shape}\")\n\n# Daily aggregation by category statistics\ncategory_stats = daily_category.groupby(['Category', 'Period']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': ['mean', 'std', 'count'],\n    'Survey ResponseID': side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = {row['Anomaly_Index']:.2f}\")\n\n# Save anomaly results\nanomaly_results.to_csv('/app/output/category_anomaly_index.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Anomaly results saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 3: FEATURE ENGINEERING\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 3: FEATURE ENGINEERING ===\")\n\n# Merge survey and shape: {merged_data.shape}\")\n\n# Engineer features\ndef engineer_features(df):\n    \"\"\"Engineer demographic and behavioral features\"\"\"\n    features = df[['Survey ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Race features (binary for major categories)\n    race_cols = ['Black or African American', 'Asian', 'White or Caucasian']\n    for race in race_cols:\n        features[f'RACE_{race.replace(\" \", \"_\").replace(\"-\", \"_\")}'] = (\n            df['Q-demos-race'].str.contains(race, na=False)\n        ).astype(int)\n\n    # Education (binary for high education)\n    features['EDUCATION_HIGH'] = (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Income (numeric - use midpoint)\n    income_map = {\n        'Less than $25,000': 20000,\n        '$25,000 - $49,999': 37500,\n        '$50,000 - $74,999': 62500,\n        '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n        '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n        '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n        'Prefer not to say': np.nan,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['INCOME_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-income'].map(income_map)\n\n    # Gender (binary for female)\n    features['GENDER_FEMALE'] = (df['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Female').astype(int)\n\n    # LGBTQ+ (binary)\n    features['LGBTQ'] = (\n        df['Q-sexual-orientation'].str.contains('LGBTQ|prefer not to say', na=False)\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Amazon usage frequency (numeric)\n    freq_map = {\n        'Less than 5 times per month': 2,\n        '5 - 10 times per month': 7.5,\n        'More than 10 times per month': 15,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AMAZON_FREQ_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = engineer_features(merged_data)\nprint(f\"Feature data shape: {feature_data.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Number of engineered features: {feature_data.shape[1] - 1}\")\n\n# Save feature data\nfeature_data.to_csv('/app/output/survey_feature_engineered.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Feature engineered data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 4: DATA AGGREGATION FOR CAUSAL ANALYSIS\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 4: DATA AGGREGATION FOR CAUSAL ANALYSIS ===\")\n\n# Get top categories for analysis\ntop_categories = list(top_surge['Category']) + list(top_slump['Category'])\nprint(f\"Analyzing {len(top_categories)} categories\")\n\n# Filter data to top categories\nanalysis_categories = analysis_data[analysis_data['Category'].isin(top_categories)].copy()\n\n# Merge with features\nanalysis_with_features = analysis_categories.merge(\n    feature_data,\n    on='Survey ResponseID',\n    how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Data with features shape: {analysis_with_features.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for intensive margin (total spend per user-category-period)\nintensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data shape: {intensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for extensive margin (purchase indicator per user-category-period)\nextensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': lambda x: (x.sum() > 0).astype(int)\n}).reset_index()\nextensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']\n\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data shape: {extensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Save aggregated data\nintensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nextensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Aggregated data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID)\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID) ===\")\n\ndef run_did_analysis(agg_data, feature_data, categories, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Run Difference-in-Differences analysis\"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for category in categories:\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing category: {category}\")\n\n        # Filter data for this category\n        cat_data = agg_data[agg_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge with features\n        cat_data = cat_data.merge(\n            feature_data,\n            on='Survey ResponseID',\n            how='inner'\n        )\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate category statistics\n        baseline_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'baseline']\n        treatment_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n        baseline_avg = baseline_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n        treatment_avg = treatment_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n\n        n_baseline = len(baseline_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(treatment_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Get anomaly index\n        anomaly_idx = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].iloc[0]\n\n        # For extensive margin, calculate purchase rates\n        if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n            baseline_rate = baseline_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n            treatment_rate = treatment_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n            n_at_risk = len(cat_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        else:\n            baseline_rate = 0\n            treatment_rate = 0\n            n_at_risk = 0\n\n        # Prepare features for DID\n        feature_cols = [col for col in feature_data.columns if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\n\n        # Simple univariate DID for each feature\n        intensive_drivers = []\n        extensive_drivers = []\n\n        for feat in feature_cols:\n            if feat in cat_data.columns:\n                # Prepare data for this feature\n                feat_data = cat_data[['Survey ResponseID', 'Period', 'Total_Spend', feat]].copy()\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n                    feat_data['Has_Purchase'] = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n\n                # Remove rows with missing feature values\n                feat_data = feat_data.dropna(subset=[feat])\n\n                if feat_data.empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create treatment indicator\n                feat_data['Treatment'] = (feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n                # Create high/low feature value indicator (median split)\n                median_val = feat_data[feat].median()\n                feat_data['Feature_High'] = (feat_data[feat] > median_val).astype(int)\n\n                # DID: Treatment * Feature_High\n                feat_data['DiD_Term'] = feat_data['Treatment'] * feat_data['Feature_High']\n\n                # Intensive margin DID\n                try:\n                    # Simple regression approach\n                    baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n                    # DID estimate\n                    did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                    # Simple p-value approximation\n                    se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Total_Spend'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                    t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                    intensive_drivers.append({\n                        'feature': feat,\n                        'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                        'p_value': float(p_value),\n                        'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n                    })\n                except:\n                    pass\n\n                # Extensive margin DID (if applicable)\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in feat_data.columns:\n                    try:\n                        baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n                        did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                        se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Has_Purchase'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                        t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                        extensive_drivers.append({\n                            'feature': feat,\n                            'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                            'p_value': float(p_value),\n                            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n                        })\n                    except:\n                        pass\n\n        # Sort drivers\n        if is_surge:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        else:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n        # Take top 3 of each\n        intensive_drivers = intensive_drivers[:3]\n        extensive_drivers = {baseline_end.date()}\")\nprint(f\"Treatment period: {treatment_start.date()} to {treatment_end.date()}\")\n\n# Add period labels\npurchases_clean['Period'] = purchases_clean['Order Date'].apply(\n    lambda x: 'baseline' if baseline_start <= x <= baseline_end\n    else 'treatment' if treatment_start <= x <= treatment_end\n    else 'other'\n)\n\n# Filter to baseline and treatment periods only\nanalysis_data = purchases_clean[purchases_clean['Period'].isin(['baseline', 'treatment'])].copy()\nprint(f\"Data for analysis: {analysis_data.shape}\")\n\n# Daily aggregation by side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n ).astype(int)\n\n    # Education (binary for high education)\n    features['EDUCATION_HIGH'] = (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Income (numeric - use midpoint)\n    income_map = {\n        'Less than $25,000': 20000,\n        '$25,000 - $49,999': 37500,\n        '$50,000 - $74,999': 62500,\n        '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n        '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n        '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n        'Prefer not to say': np.nan,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Data with features shape: {analysis_with_features.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for intensive margin (total spend per user-category-period)\nintensive_agg = 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data shape: {intensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for extensive margin (purchase indicator per user-category-period)\nextensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': lambda x: (x.sum() > 0).astype(int)\n}).reset_index()\nextensive_agg.columns = ['Survey data\nintensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nextensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Aggregated data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID)\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID) ===\")\n\ndef run_did_analysis(agg_data, feature_data, categories, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Run Difference-in-Differences analysis\"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for category in categories:\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing category: {category}\")\n\n        # Filter data for this category\n        cat_data = agg_data[agg_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge with features\n        cat_data = cat_data.merge(\n            feature_data,\n            on='Survey ResponseID',\n            how='inner'\n        )\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate category statistics\n        baseline_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'baseline']\n        treatment_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n        baseline_avg = baseline_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n        treatment_avg = treatment_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n\n        n_baseline = len(baseline_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(treatment_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Get anomaly index\n        anomaly_idx = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].iloc[0]\n\n        # For extensive margin, calculate purchase rates\n        if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n            baseline_rate = baseline_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n            treatment_rate = treatment_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n            n_at_risk = len(cat_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        else:\n            baseline_rate = 0\n            treatment_rate = 0\n            n_at_risk = 0\n\n        # Prepare features for DID\n        feature_cols = [col for col in feature_data.columns if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\n\n        # Simple univariate DID for each feature\n        intensive_drivers = []\n        extensive_drivers = []\n\n        for feat in feature_cols:\n            if feat in cat_data.columns:\n                # Prepare data for this feature\n                feat_data = cat_data[['Survey ResponseID', 'Period', 'Total_Spend', feat]].copy()\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n                    feat_data['Has_Purchase'] = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n\n                # Remove rows with missing feature values\n                feat_data = feat_data.dropna(subset=[feat])\n\n                if feat_data.empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create treatment indicator\n                feat_data['Treatment'] = (feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n                # Create high/low feature value indicator (median split)\n                median_val = feat_data[feat].median()\n                feat_data['Feature_High'] = (feat_data[feat] > median_val).astype(int)\n\n                # DID: Treatment * Feature_High\n                feat_data['DiD_Term'] = feat_data['Treatment'] * feat_data['Feature_High']\n\n                # Intensive margin DID\n                try:\n                    # Simple regression approach\n                    baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n                    # DID estimate\n                    did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                    # Simple p-value approximation\n                    se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Total_Spend'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                    t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                    intensive_drivers.append({\n                        'feature': feat,\n                        'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                        'p_value': float(p_value),\n                        'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n                    })\n                except:\n                    pass\n\n                # Extensive margin DID (if applicable)\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in feat_data.columns:\n                    try:\n                        baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n                        did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                        se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Has_Purchase'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                        t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                        extensive_drivers.append({\n                            'feature': feat,\n                            'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                            'p_value': float(p_value),\n                            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n                        })\n                    except:\n                        pass\n\n        # Sort drivers\n        if is_surge:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        else:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n        # Take top 3 of each\n        intensive_drivers = intensive_drivers[:3]\n        extensive_drivers = extensive_drivers[:3]\n\n        # Add to results\n        results.append({\n            'category': category,\n            'anomaly_index': float(anomaly_idx),\n            'baseline_avg_spend': dates: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\n\n# Clean numeric columns\npurchases_clean['Purchase Price Per Unit'] = pd.to_numeric(\n    purchases_clean['Purchase Price Per Unit'], purchases_clean.dropna(subset=['Purchase Price Per Unit', 'Category'])\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean.dropna(subset=['Survey ResponseID'])\n\n# Calculate total purchase amount\npurchases_clean['Total_Amount'] = (\n    purchases_clean['Purchase Price Per Unit'] * purchases_clean['Quantity']\n)\n\nprint(f\"After cleaning purchase data: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\n\n# Filter data to 2019-2020 period\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean[\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] >= '2019-01-01') &\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] <= '2020-12-31')\n]\n\nprint(f\"After 2: ANOMALY DETECTION\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 2: ANOMALY DETECTION ===\")\n\n# Define baseline and treatment periods\nbaseline_start = datetime(2020, 1, category statistics\ncategory_stats = daily_category.groupby(['Category', 'Period']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': ['mean', 'std', 'count'],\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'mean'\n}).reset_index()\n\ncategory_stats.columns = ['Category', 'Period', 'avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers']\n\n# Pivot to get baseline and treatment stats side by side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = shape: {merged_data.shape}\")\n\n# Engineer features\ndef engineer_features(df):\n    \"\"\"Engineer demographic and behavioral features\"\"\"\n    features = df[['Survey ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Race features (binary for major categories)\n    race_cols = ['Black or African American', 'Asian', 'White or Caucasian']\n    for race in race_cols:\n        features[f'RACE_{race.replace(\" \", \"_\").replace(\"-\", \"_\")}'] = (\n            df['Q-demos-race'].str.contains(race, na=False)\n        ).astype(int)\n\n    # Education (binary for high education)\n    features['EDUCATION_HIGH'] = (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Income (numeric - use midpoint)\n    income_map = {\n        'Less than $25,000': 20000,\n        '$25,000 - $49,999': 37500,\n        '$50,000 - $74,999': 62500,\n        '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n        '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n        '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n        'Prefer not to say': np.nan,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['INCOME_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-income'].map(income_map)\n\n    # Gender (binary for female)\n    features['GENDER_FEMALE'] = (df['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Female').astype(int)\n\n    # LGBTQ+ (binary)\n    features['LGBTQ'] = (\n        df['Q-sexual-orientation'].str.contains('LGBTQ|prefer not to say', na=False)\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Amazon usage frequency (numeric)\n    freq_map = {\n        'Less than 5 times per month': 2,\n        '5 - 10 times per month': 7.5,\n        'More than 10 times per month': 15,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AMAZON_FREQ_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = engineer_features(merged_data)\nprint(f\"Feature data shape: {feature_data.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Number of engineered features: {feature_data.shape[1] - 1}\")\n\n# Save feature how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Data with features shape: {analysis_with_features.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for intensive margin (total spend per user-category-period)\nintensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data shape: {intensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for extensive margin (purchase indicator per user-category-period)\nextensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': lambda x: (x.sum() > 0).astype(int)\n}).reset_index()\nextensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']\n\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data shape: {extensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Save aggregated data\nintensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nextensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Aggregated data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID)\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID) ===\")\n\ndef run_did_analysis(agg_data, feature_data, categories, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Run Difference-in-Differences analysis\"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for category in categories:\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing category: {category}\")\n\n        # Filter data for this category\n        cat_data = agg_data[agg_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge with features\n        cat_data = cat_data.merge(\n            feature_data,\n            on='Survey ResponseID',\n            how='inner'\n        )\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate category statistics\n        baseline_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'baseline']\n        treatment_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n        baseline_avg = baseline_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n        treatment_avg = treatment_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n\n        n_baseline = len(baseline_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(treatment_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Get anomaly index\n        anomaly_idx = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].iloc[0]\n\n        # For extensive margin, calculate purchase rates\n        if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n            baseline_rate = baseline_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n            treatment_rate = treatment_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n            n_at_risk = len(cat_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        else:\n            baseline_rate = 0\n            treatment_rate = 0\n            n_at_risk = 0\n\n        # Prepare features for DID\n        feature_cols = [col for col in feature_data.columns if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\n\n        # Simple univariate DID for each feature\n        intensive_drivers = []\n        extensive_drivers = []\n\n        for feat in feature_cols:\n            if feat in cat_data.columns:\n                # Prepare data for this feature\n                feat_data = cat_data[['Survey ResponseID', 'Period', 'Total_Spend', feat]].copy()\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n                    feat_data['Has_Purchase'] = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n\n                # Remove rows with missing feature values\n                feat_data = feat_data.dropna(subset=[feat])\n\n                if feat_data.empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create treatment indicator\n                feat_data['Treatment'] = (feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n                # Create high/low feature value indicator (median split)\n                median_val = feat_data[feat].median()\n                feat_data['Feature_High'] = (feat_data[feat] > median_val).astype(int)\n\n                # DID: Treatment * Feature_High\n                feat_data['DiD_Term'] = feat_data['Treatment'] * feat_data['Feature_High']\n\n                # Intensive margin DID\n                try:\n                    # Simple regression approach\n                    baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n                    # DID estimate\n                    did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                    # Simple p-value approximation\n                    se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Total_Spend'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                    t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                    intensive_drivers.append({\n                        'feature': feat,\n                        'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                        'p_value': float(p_value),\n                        'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n                    })\n                except:\n                    pass\n\n                # Extensive margin DID (if applicable)\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in feat_data.columns:\n                    try:\n                        baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n                        did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                        se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Has_Purchase'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                        t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                        extensive_drivers.append({\n                            'feature': feat,\n                            'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                            'p_value': float(p_value),\n                            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n                        })\n                    except:\n                        pass\n\n        # Sort drivers\n        if is_surge:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        else:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n        # Take top 3 of each\n        intensive_drivers = intensive_drivers[:3]\n        extensive_drivers = extensive_drivers[:3]\n\n        # Add to results\n        results.append({\n            'category': category,\n            'anomaly_index': float(anomaly_idx),\n            'baseline_avg_spend': float(baseline_avg),\n            'treatment_avg_spend': float(treatment_avg),\n            'n_purchasers_baseline': int(n_baseline),\n            'n_purchasers_treatment': int(n_treatment),\n            'baseline_purchase_rate': float(baseline_rate),\n            'treatment_purchase_rate': float(treatment_rate),\n            'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n            'intensive_margin': intensive_drivers,\n            'extensive_margin': extensive_drivers\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n# Run analysis for surge categories\nprint(\"\\n--- Surge Categories ---\")\nsurge_results = run_did_analysis(\n    intensive_agg,\n    feature_data,\n    top_surge['Category'].tolist(),\n    is_surge=True\n)\n\n# Run analysis for slump categories\nprint(\"\\n--- Slump Categories ---\")\nslump_results = run_did_analysis(\n    intensive_agg,\n    feature_data,\n    top_slump['Category'].tolist(),\n    is_surge=False\n)\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 6: GENERATE FINAL REPORT\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 6: GENERATE FINAL REPORT ===\")\n\n# Create final report\nreport = {\n    'metadata': {\n        'baseline_start': '01-01-2020',\n        'baseline_end': '02-29-2020',\n        'treatment_start': '03-01-2020',\n        'treatment_end': '03-31-2020',\n        'total_features_analyzed': feature_data.shape[1] - 1\n    },\n    'surge_categories': surge_results,\n    'slump_categories': slump_results,\n    'summary': {\n        'surge': {\n            'total_categories': side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = ).astype(int)\n\n    # Education (binary for high education)\n    features['EDUCATION_HIGH'] = (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n {\n        'Less than 5 times per month': 2,\n        '5 - 10 times per month': 7.5,\n        'More than 10 times per month': 15,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AMAZON_FREQ_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = engineer_features(merged_data)\nprint(f\"Feature data shape: {feature_data.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Number of engineered features: {feature_data.shape[1] - 1}\")\n\n# Save feature how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Data with features shape: {analysis_with_features.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for intensive margin (total spend per user-category-period)\nintensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data shape: {intensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for extensive margin (purchase indicator per user-category-period)\nextensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': lambda x: (x.sum() > 0).astype(int)\n}).reset_index()\nextensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']\n\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data shape: {extensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Save aggregated data\nintensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nextensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Aggregated data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID)\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID) ===\")\n\ndef run_did_analysis(agg_data, feature_data, categories, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Run Difference-in-Differences analysis\"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for category in categories:\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing category: {category}\")\n\n        # Filter data for this category\n        cat_data = agg_data[agg_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge with features\n        cat_data = cat_data.merge(\n            feature_data,\n            on='Survey ResponseID',\n            how='inner'\n        )\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate category statistics\n        baseline_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'baseline']\n        treatment_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n        baseline_avg = baseline_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n        treatment_avg = treatment_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n\n        n_baseline = len(baseline_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(treatment_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Get anomaly index\n        anomaly_idx = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].iloc[0]\n\n        # For extensive margin, calculate purchase rates\n        if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n            baseline_rate = baseline_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n            treatment_rate = treatment_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n            n_at_risk = len(cat_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        else:\n            baseline_rate = 0\n            treatment_rate = 0\n            n_at_risk = 0\n\n        # Prepare features for DID\n        feature_cols = [col for col in feature_data.columns if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\n\n        # Simple univariate DID for each feature\n        intensive_drivers = []\n        extensive_drivers = []\n\n        for feat in feature_cols:\n            if feat in cat_data.columns:\n                # Prepare data for this feature\n                feat_data = cat_data[['Survey ResponseID', 'Period', 'Total_Spend', feat]].copy()\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n                    feat_data['Has_Purchase'] = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n\n                # Remove rows with missing feature values\n                feat_data = feat_data.dropna(subset=[feat])\n\n                if feat_data.empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create treatment indicator\n                feat_data['Treatment'] = (feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n                # Create high/low feature value indicator (median split)\n                median_val = feat_data[feat].median()\n                feat_data['Feature_High'] = (feat_data[feat] > median_val).astype(int)\n\n                # DID: Treatment * Feature_High\n                feat_data['DiD_Term'] = feat_data['Treatment'] * feat_data['Feature_High']\n\n                # Intensive margin DID\n                try:\n                    # Simple regression approach\n                    baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n                    # DID estimate\n                    did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                    # Simple p-value approximation\n                    se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Total_Spend'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                    t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                    intensive_drivers.append({\n                        'feature': feat,\n                        'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                        'p_value': float(p_value),\n                        'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n                    })\n                except:\n                    pass\n\n                # Extensive margin DID (if applicable)\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in feat_data.columns:\n                    try:\n                        baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n                        did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                        se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Has_Purchase'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                        t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                        extensive_drivers.append({\n                            'feature': feat,\n                            'did_estimate': float(did_estimate),\n                            'p_value': float(p_value),\n                            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n                        })\n                    except:\n                        pass\n\n        # Sort drivers\n        if is_surge:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        else:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n        # Take top 3 of each\n        intensive_drivers = intensive_drivers[:3]\n        extensive_drivers = Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Income (numeric - use midpoint)\n    income_map = {\n        'Less (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = engineer_features(merged_data)\nprint(f\"Feature data shape: {feature_data.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Number   return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category statistics\ncategory_stats = daily_category.groupby(['Category', 'Period']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': ['mean', 'std', 'count'],\n    'Survey ResponseID': side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results - remove any rows with empty Category\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'].notna() & (anomaly_results['Category'] != '')]\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = anomaly_results.tail(10)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTop 10 Surge Categories (Anomaly Index > 0):\")\nfor _, row in top_surge.iterrows():\n    print(f\"  {row['Category']}: {row['Anomaly_Index']:.2f}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nTop 10 Slump Categories (Anomaly Index < 0):\")\nfor _, row in    features = df[['Survey ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years':  # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Race features (binary for major categories)\n    race_cols = ['Black or African American', 'Asian', 'White or Caucasian']\n    for race in race_cols:\n        features[f'RACE_{race.replace(\" \", \"_\").replace(\"-\", \"_\")}'] = (\n            df['Q-demos-race'].str.contains(race, $74,999': 62500,\n        '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n        '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n        '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n        'Prefer not to say': np.nan,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['INCOME_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-income'].map(income_map)\n\n    # Gender (binary for female)\n    features['GENDER_FEMALE'] = (df['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Female').astype(int)\n\n    # LGBTQ+ (binary)\n    features['LGBTQ'] = (\n        df['Q-sexual-orientation'].str.contains('LGBTQ|prefer not to say', na=False)\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Amazon usage frequency (numeric)\n    freq_map = {\n        'Less than 5 times per month': 2,\n        '5 - 10 times per month': 7.5,\n        'More than 10 times per month': 15,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AMAZON_FREQ_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with ['Q-demos-hispanic', 'Q-demos-race', 'Q-demos-education',\n                   'Q-demos-income', 'Q-demos-gender', 'Q-sexual-orientation',\n                   'Q-demos-state']\n\nfor col in categorical_cols:\n    if col in survey_clean.columns:\n        survey_clean[col] = survey_clean[col].fillna('Unknown')\n\n# Clean age column - extract numeric value\nsurvey_clean['Q-demos-age'] = survey_clean['Q-demos-age'].fillna('Unknown')\n\n# Clean amazon usage columns\namazon_cols = ['Q-amazon-use-howmany', 'Q-amazon-use-hh-size', 'Q-amazon-use-how-oft']\nfor col in amazon_cols:\n    if col in survey_clean.columns:\n        survey_clean[col] = survey_clean[col].fillna('Unknown')\n\n# Remove rows with missing ResponseID\nsurvey_clean = survey_clean.dropna(subset=['Survey exact duplicates\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean.drop_duplicates()\nprint(f\"After removing duplicate rows: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\n\n# Clean date column\npurchases_clean['Order Date'] = pd.to_datetime(purchases_clean['Order Date'], errors='coerce')\n\n# Remove rows with invalid dates\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean.dropna(subset=['Order Date'])\nprint(f\"After removing invalid purchases_clean.dropna(subset=['Purchase Price Per Unit', 'Category'])\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean.dropna(subset=['Survey ResponseID'])\n\n# Calculate total purchase amount\npurchases_clean['Total_Amount'] = (\n    purchases_clean['Purchase Price Per Unit'] * purchases_clean['Quantity']\n)\n\nprint(f\"After cleaning purchase data: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\n\n# Filter data to 2019-2020 period\npurchases_clean = purchases_clean[\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] >= '2019-01-01') &\n    (purchases_clean['Order Date'] <= '2020-12-31')\n]\n\nprint(f\"After filtering to 2019-2020: {purchases_clean.shape}\")\n\n# Save cleaned datasets\nsurvey_clean.to_csv('/app/output/survey_cleaned.csv', 2: ANOMALY DETECTION\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 2: ANOMALY DETECTION ===\")\n\n# Define baseline and treatment periods\nbaseline_start = datetime(2020, 1, 1)\nbaseline_end = datetime(2020, 2, 29)\ntreatment_start = datetime(2020, 3, 1)\ntreatment_end = datetime(2020, 3, 31)\n\nprint(f\"Baseline period: {baseline_start.date()} to {baseline_end.date()}\")\nprint(f\"Treatment period: {treatment_start.date()} to {treatment_end.date()}\")\n\n# Add period labels\npurchases_clean['Period'] = purchases_clean['Order Date'].apply(\n    lambda x: 'baseline' if baseline_start <= x <= baseline_end\n    else 'treatment' if treatment_start <= x <= treatment_end\n    else 'other'\n)\n\n# Filter to baseline and treatment periods only\nanalysis_data = purchases_clean[purchases_clean['Period'].isin(['baseline', 'treatment'])].copy()\nprint(f\"Data for analysis: {analysis_data.shape}\")\n\n# Daily aggregation by category\ndaily_category = analysis_data.groupby(\n    ['Category', 'Order Date', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum',\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'nunique'\n}).reset_index()\n\n# Calculate category statistics\ncategory_stats = daily_category.groupby(['Category', 'Period']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': ['mean', 'std', 'count'],\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'mean'\n}).reset_index()\n\ncategory_stats.columns = ['Category', 'Period', 'avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers']\n\n# Pivot to get baseline and treatment stats side by side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results - remove any rows with empty Category\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'].notna() & (anomaly_results['Category'] != '')]\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = anomaly_results.tail(10)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTop 10 Surge Categories (Anomaly Index > 0):\")\nfor _, row in top_surge.iterrows():\n    print(f\"  {row['Category']}: {row['Anomaly_Index']:.2f}\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nTop 10 Slump Categories (Anomaly Index < 0):\")\nfor _, row in top_slump.iterrows():\n    print(f\"  {row['Category']}: {row['Anomaly_Index']:.2f}\")\n\n# Save anomaly results\nanomaly_results.to_csv('/app/output/category_anomaly_index.csv', 3: FEATURE ENGINEERING ===\")\n\n# Merge survey and purchases for feature engineering\nmerged_data = analysis_data.merge(\n    survey_clean,\n    left_on='Survey ResponseID',\n    right_on='Survey ResponseID',\n    how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Merged data shape: {merged_data.shape}\")\n\n# Engineer features\ndef engineer_features(df):\n    \"\"\"Engineer demographic and behavioral features\"\"\"\n    features = df[['Survey ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Race features (binary for major categories)\n    race_cols = ['Black or African American', 'Asian', 'White or Caucasian']\n    for race in race_cols:\n        features[f'RACE_{race.replace(\" \", \"_\").replace(\"-\", \"_\")}'] = (\n            df['Q-demos-race'].str.contains(race, na=False)\n        ).astype(int)\n\n    # Education (binary for high education)\n    features['EDUCATION_HIGH'] = (\n        df['Q-demos-education'].str.contains(\n            'Graduate|Bachelor', na=False\n        )\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Income (numeric - use midpoint)\n    income_map = {\n        'Less than $25,000': 20000,\n        '$25,000 - $49,999': 37500,\n        '$50,000 - $74,999': 62500,\n        '$75,000 - $99,999': 87500,\n        '$100,000 - $149,999': 125000,\n        '$150,000 or more': 175000,\n        'Prefer not to say': np.nan,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['INCOME_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-income'].map(income_map)\n\n    # Gender (binary for female)\n    features['GENDER_FEMALE'] = (df['Q-demos-gender'] == 'Female').astype(int)\n\n    # LGBTQ+ (binary)\n    features['LGBTQ'] = (\n        df['Q-sexual-orientation'].str.contains('LGBTQ|prefer not to say', na=False)\n    ).astype(int)\n\n    # Amazon usage frequency (numeric)\n    freq_map = {\n        'Less than 5 times per month': 2,\n        '5 - 10 times per month': 7.5,\n        'More than 10 times per month': 15,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AMAZON_FREQ_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-amazon-use-how-oft'].map(freq_map)\n\n    # Household size (numeric)\n    hh_map = {\n        '1 (just me!)': 1,\n        '2': 2,\n        '3': 3,\n        '4+': 4,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['HOUSEHOLD_SIZE'] = df['Q-amazon-use-hh-size'].map(hh_map)\n\n    # Substance use (binary)\n    features['SMOKES'] = (df['Q-substance-use-cigarettes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['DRINKS_ALCOHOL'] = (df['Q-substance-use-alcohol'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Health conditions (binary)\n    features['HAS_DIABETES'] = (df['Q-personal-diabetes'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n    features['WHEELCHAIR_USER'] = (df['Q-personal-wheelchair'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Fill missing values with median for numeric, mode for categorical\n    for col in features.columns:\n        if col != 'Survey ResponseID':\n            if features[col].dtype in ['int64', 'float64']:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].median())\n            else:\n                features[col] = features[col].fillna(features[col].mode()[0] if not features[col].mode().empty else 0)\n\n    return features\n\n# Engineer features\nfeature_data = engineer_features(merged_data)\nprint(f\"Feature data shape: {feature_data.shape}\")\nprint(f\"Number of engineered features: {feature_data.shape[1] - 1}\")\n\n# Save feature data\nfeature_data.to_csv('/app/output/survey_feature_engineered.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Feature engineered data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 4: DATA AGGREGATION FOR CAUSAL ANALYSIS\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 4: DATA AGGREGATION FOR CAUSAL ANALYSIS ===\")\n\n# Get top categories for analysis\ntop_categories = list(top_surge['Category']) + list(top_slump['Category'])\nprint(f\"Analyzing {len(top_categories)} categories\")\n\n# Filter data to top categories\nanalysis_categories = analysis_data[analysis_data['Category'].isin(top_categories)].copy()\n\n# Merge with features\nanalysis_with_features = analysis_categories.merge(\n    feature_data,\n    on='Survey ResponseID',\n    how='left'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Data with features shape: {analysis_with_features.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for intensive margin (total spend per user-category-period)\nintensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum'\n}).reset_index()\nintensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Total_Spend']\n\nprint(f\"Intensive margin data shape: {intensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Aggregate for extensive margin (purchase indicator per user-category-period)\nextensive_agg = analysis_with_features.groupby(\n    ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': lambda x: (x.sum() > 0).astype(int)\n}).reset_index()\nextensive_agg.columns = ['Survey ResponseID', 'Category', 'Period', 'Has_Purchase']\n\nprint(f\"Extensive margin data shape: {extensive_agg.shape}\")\n\n# Save aggregated data\nintensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_intensive.csv', index=False)\nextensive_agg.to_csv('/app/output/user_category_period_aggregated_extensive.csv', index=False)\nprint(\"\\n✓ Aggregated data saved\")\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID)\n# ==========================================\n\nprint(\"\\n=== STEP 5: CAUSAL ANALYSIS (DID) ===\")\n\ndef run_did_analysis(agg_data, feature_data, categories, is_surge=True):\n    \"\"\"Run Difference-in-Differences analysis\"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for category in categories:\n        print(f\"\\nAnalyzing category: {category}\")\n\n        # Filter data for this category\n        cat_data = agg_data[agg_data['Category'] == category].copy()\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Merge with features\n        cat_data = cat_data.merge(\n            feature_data,\n            on='Survey ResponseID',\n            how='inner'\n        )\n\n        if cat_data.empty:\n            continue\n\n        # Calculate category statistics\n        baseline_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'baseline']\n        treatment_data = cat_data[cat_data['Period'] == 'treatment']\n\n        baseline_avg = baseline_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n        treatment_avg = treatment_data['Total_Spend'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n\n        n_baseline = len(baseline_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        n_treatment = len(treatment_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n\n        # Get anomaly index\n        anomaly_idx = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'] == category]['Anomaly_Index'].iloc[0]\n\n        # For extensive margin, calculate purchase rates\n        if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n            baseline_rate = baseline_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not baseline_data.empty else 0\n            treatment_rate = treatment_data['Has_Purchase'].mean() if not treatment_data.empty else 0\n            n_at_risk = len(cat_data['Survey ResponseID'].unique())\n        else:\n            baseline_rate = 0\n            treatment_rate = 0\n            n_at_risk = 0\n\n        # Prepare features for DID\n        feature_cols = [col for col in feature_data.columns if col != 'Survey ResponseID']\n\n        # Simple univariate DID for each feature\n        intensive_drivers = []\n        extensive_drivers = []\n\n        for feat in feature_cols:\n            if feat in cat_data.columns:\n                # Prepare data for this feature\n                feat_data = cat_data[['Survey ResponseID', 'Period', 'Total_Spend', feat]].copy()\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in cat_data.columns:\n                    feat_data['Has_Purchase'] = cat_data['Has_Purchase']\n\n                # Remove rows with missing feature values\n                feat_data = feat_data.dropna(subset=[feat])\n\n                if feat_data.empty:\n                    continue\n\n                # Create treatment indicator\n                feat_data['Treatment'] = (feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment').astype(int)\n\n                # Create high/low feature value indicator (median split)\n                median_val = feat_data[feat].median()\n                feat_data['Feature_High'] = (feat_data[feat] > median_val).astype(int)\n\n                # DID: Treatment * Feature_High\n                feat_data['DiD_Term'] = feat_data['Treatment'] * feat_data['Feature_High']\n\n                # Intensive margin DID\n                try:\n                    # Simple regression approach\n                    baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n                    treatment_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'treatment') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Total_Spend'].mean()\n\n                    # DID estimate\n                    did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                    # Simple p-value approximation\n                    se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Total_Spend'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                    t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                    intensive_drivers.append({\n                        'feature': feat,\n                        'did_estimate': float(did_estimate) if not np.isnan(did_estimate) else None,\n                        'p_value': float(p_value) if not np.isnan(p_value) else None,\n                        'method': 'Univariate DiD'\n                    })\n                except:\n                    pass\n\n                # Extensive margin DID (if applicable)\n                if 'Has_Purchase' in feat_data.columns:\n                    try:\n                        baseline_high = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 1)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        baseline_low = feat_data[(feat_data['Period'] == 'baseline') & (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n                        treatment_high = (feat_data['Feature_High'] == 0)]['Has_Purchase'].mean()\n\n                        did_estimate = (treatment_high - baseline_high) - (treatment_low - baseline_low)\n\n                        se = np.sqrt(feat_data['Has_Purchase'].var() / len(feat_data))\n                        t_stat = did_estimate / se if se > 0 else 0\n                        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(t_stat)))\n\n                        extensive_drivers.append({\n                            'feature': feat,\n                            'did_estimate': float(did_estimate) if not np.isnan(did_estimate) else None,\n                            'p_value': float(p_value) if not np.isnan(p_value) else None,\n                            'method': 'Multivariate Heterogeneous DiD'\n                        })\n                    except:\n                        pass\n\n        # Sort drivers (filter out None values first)\n        intensive_drivers = [d for d in intensive_drivers if d['did_estimate'] is not None]\n        extensive_drivers = [d for d in extensive_drivers if d['did_estimate'] is not None]\n\n        if is_surge:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'], reverse=True)\n        else:\n            intensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n            extensive_drivers.sort(key=lambda x: x['did_estimate'])\n\n        # Take top 3 of each\n        intensive_drivers = intensive_drivers[:3]\n        extensive_drivers = extensive_drivers[:3]\n\n        # Add to results\n        results.append({\n            'category': category,\n            'anomaly_index': float(anomaly_idx),\n            'baseline_avg_spend': float(baseline_avg),\n            'treatment_avg_spend': float(treatment_avg),\n            'n_purchasers_baseline': int(n_baseline),\n            'n_purchasers_treatment': int(n_treatment),\n            'baseline_purchase_rate': float(baseline_rate),\n            'treatment_purchase_rate': float(treatment_rate),\n            'n_at_risk': int(n_at_risk),\n            'intensive_margin': intensive_drivers,\n            'extensive_margin': extensive_drivers\n        })\n\n    return results\n\n# Run analysis for surge categories\nprint(\"\\n--- Surge Categories ---\")\nsurge_results = run_did_analysis(\n    intensive_agg,\n    feature_data,\n    top_surge['Category'].tolist(),\n    is_surge=True\n)\n\n# Run analysis for slump categories\nprint(\"\\n--- Slump Categories ---\")\nslump_results = run_did_analysis(\n    intensive_agg,\n    feature_data,\n    top_slump['Category'].tolist(),\n    is_surge=False\n)\n\n# ==========================================\n# STEP 6: lambda x: 'baseline' if baseline_start <= x <= baseline_end\n    else 'treatment' if treatment_start <= x <= treatment_end\n    else 'other'\n)\n\n# Filter to baseline and treatment periods only\nanalysis_data = purchases_clean[purchases_clean['Period'].isin(['baseline', 'treatment'])].copy()\nprint(f\"Data for analysis: {analysis_data.shape}\")\n\n# Daily aggregation by category\ndaily_category = analysis_data.groupby(\n    ['Category', 'Order Date', 'Period']\n).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': 'sum',\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'nunique'\n}).reset_index()\n\n# Calculate category statistics\ncategory_stats = daily_category.groupby(['Category', 'Period']).agg({\n    'Total_Amount': ['mean', 'std', 'count'],\n    'Survey ResponseID': 'mean'\n}).reset_index()\n\ncategory_stats.columns = ['Category', 'Period', 'avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers']\n\n# Pivot to get baseline and treatment stats side by side\ncategory_pivot = category_stats.pivot(index='Category', columns='Period', values=['avg_amount', 'std_amount', 'n_days', 'avg_customers'])\ncategory_pivot = category_pivot.fillna(0)\n\n# Calculate anomaly index\ndef calculate_anomaly_index(row):\n    baseline_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'baseline')]\n    treatment_avg = row[('avg_amount', 'treatment')]\n\n    if baseline_avg <= 0:\n        return 0\n\n    # Calculate percentage change\n    pct_change = (treatment_avg - baseline_avg) / baseline_avg\n\n    # Normalize to -100 to 100 scale\n    # Scale factor determines sensitivity\n    scale_factor = 2.0\n    anomaly_index = max(-100, min(100, pct_change * scale_factor * 100))\n\n    return anomaly_index\n\ncategory_pivot['Anomaly_Index'] = category_pivot.apply(calculate_anomaly_index, axis=1)\n\n# Create anomaly results - remove any rows with empty Category\nanomaly_results = category_pivot.reset_index()[['Category', 'Anomaly_Index']].copy()\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results[anomaly_results['Category'].notna() & (anomaly_results['Category'] != '')]\nanomaly_results = anomaly_results.sort_values('Anomaly_Index', ascending=False)\n\n# Get top 10 surge and top 10 slump categories\ntop_surge = anomaly_results.head(10)\ntop_slump = anomaly_results.tail(10)\n\nprint(f\"\\nTop 10 Surge Categories (Anomaly Index > 0):\")\nfor _, row in top_surge.iterrows():\n    category = row['Category'] if pd.notna(row['Category']) and row['Category'] != '' else 'Unknown'\n    print(f\"  {category}: row['Category'] != '' else 'Unknown'\n    print(f\"  {category}: {row['Anomaly_Index']:.2f}\")\n\n# Save anomaly results\nanomaly_results.to_csv('/app/output/category_anomaly_index.csv', 3: FEATURE ENGINEERING ===\")\n\n# Merge survey and purchases for feature engineering\nmerged_data = analysis_data.merge(\n    survey_clean,\n    left_on='Survey ResponseID',\n    right_on='Survey    features = df[['Survey ResponseID']].drop_duplicates()\n\n    # Age feature - numeric\n    age_map = {\n        '18 - 24 years': 21,\n        '25 - 34 years': 29.5,\n        '35 - 44 years': 39.5,\n        '45 - 54 years': 49.5,\n        '55 - 64 years': 59.5,\n        '65 and older': 70,\n        'Unknown': np.nan\n    }\n    features['AGE_NUMERIC'] = df['Q-demos-age'].map(age_map)\n\n    # Hispanic (binary)\n    features['HISPANIC'] = (df['Q-demos-hispanic'] == 'Yes').astype(int)\n\n    # Race features (binary for major categories)\n    race_cols = ['Black or African American', 'Asian', 'White or Caucasian']\n    for race in race_cols:\n        features[f'RACE_{race.replace(\" \", \"_\").replace(\"-\", \"_\")}'] = (\n            df['Q-demos-race'].str.contains(race,
